 155:     (0x08, 0xEE010F10, "mcr 15,0,r0,cr1,cr0,0", "MMU OFF -> VA == PA"),
 156: ]
 157: MMU_NOP = [
 158:     (0x00, NOP, NOPTXT, "arm T: MMU LEFT ON -- the negative control"),
 159:     (0x04, NOP, NOPTXT, ""),
 160:     (0x08, NOP, NOPTXT, ""),
 161: ]
 162: 
 163: 
 164: def prologue(sentinel_word, sentinel_txt):
 165:     return [
 166:     (0x0c, 0xEE113F10, "mrc 15,0,r3,cr1,cr0,0", "C2: read CP15 c1 BACK -- measures MMU state"),
 167:     (0x10, 0xE3A00442, "mov r0,#0x42000000",    "footbridge base (machine_cats.c:63-64)"),
 168:     (0x14, 0xE3A04A09, "mov r4,#0x9000",        "scratch RAM"),
 169:     (0x18, 0xE5945000, "ldr r5,[r4]",           "C1 liveness: plain RAM, expect 0x11223344"),
 170:     (0x1c, 0xE5906000, "ldr r6,[r0]",           "A1: VENDOR_ID, expect 0x1011 (RAM/absent: 0)"),
 171:     (0x20, 0xE5942004, "ldr r2,[r4,#4]",        "0x12345678 out of RAM"),
 172:     (0x24, 0xE5802300, "str r2,[r0,#768]",      "TIMER_1_LOAD <- 0x12345678"),
 173:     (0x28, 0xE5907300, "ldr r7,[r0,#768]",      "A2: expect 0x00345678 -- the handler's own mask"),
 174:     (0x2c, 0xE3A01080, "mov r1,#0x80",          "TIMER_ENABLE (dc21285reg.h:367)"),
 175:     (0x30, 0xE5801308, "str r1,[r0,#776]",      "TIMER_1_CONTROL <- TIMER_ENABLE"),
 176:     (0x34, 0xE3A01001, "mov r1,#1",             ""),
 177:     (0x38, 0xE5801300, "str r1,[r0,#768]",      "TIMER_1_LOAD <- 1  => 50 MHz, modulus 1"),
 178:     (0x3c, sentinel_word, sentinel_txt,         "the sentinel"),
 179:     (0x40, 0xE3A0A000, "mov sl,#0",             "r10: delivered-tick count"),
 180:     (0x44, 0xE3A0B000, "mov fp,#0",             "r11: guest iteration count"),
 181:     ]
 182: 
 183: 
 184: #  The free-running loop.  Every word is planted; NONE of it is stepped in arm A.
 185: LOOP = [
 186:     (0x48, 0xE5808304, "str r8,[r0,#772]",  "outer: TIMER_1_VALUE <- sentinel"),
 187:     (0x4c, 0xE28BB001, "add fp,fp,#1",      "inner: iterations++"),
 188:     (0x50, 0xE584B024, "str fp,[r4,#36]",   "publish iterations   -> 0x9024"),
 189:     (0x54, 0xE5901000, "ldr r1,[r0]",       "VENDOR_ID, EVERY iteration"),
 190:     (0x58, 0xE584102C, "str r1,[r4,#44]",   "publish VENDOR_ID    -> 0x902c"),
 191:     (0x5c, 0xE5909304, "ldr r9,[r0,#772]",  "read TIMER_1_VALUE"),
 192:     (0x60, 0xE5849028, "str r9,[r4,#40]",   "publish it           -> 0x9028"),
 193:     (0x64, 0xE3590000, "cmps r9,#0",        "0 == a tick was delivered"),
 194:     (0x68, 0x1AFFFFF7, "bne 0x804c",        "sentinel intact -> keep polling"),
 195:     (0x6c, 0xE28AA001, "add sl,sl,#1",      "a tick was delivered: count it"),
 196:     (0x70, 0xE584A020, "str sl,[r4,#32]",   "publish tick count   -> 0x9020"),
 197:     (0x74, 0xEAFFFFF3, "b 0x8048",          "re-arm the sentinel"),
 198: ]
 199: LANDING = (0x78, NOP, NOPTXT, "landing pad -- planted, never reached")
 200: #  (#545: the KILL word -- a +0x18c read that used to end the process -- is retired; see run_legs)
 201: 
 202: SENT_A = (0xE3A088AB, "mov r8,#0xab0000")     # 0x00AB0000
 203: SENT_E = (0xE3A08000, "mov r8,#0")            # arm E: the ONE immediate that differs
 204: 
 205: PROLOGUE_N = 18          # instructions from 0x00 to 0x44 inclusive
 206: TIMERMSG   = "asked for more ticks than a host period can deliver"
 207: 
 208: 
 209: class Session:
 210:     """One emulator process on a cold debugger."""
 211: 
 212:     def __init__(self, emul="cats"):           # #545: leg H1 passes an unknown name
 478: #  #545: THE HARNESS LEGS, replacing arm K.  Arm K read +0x18c, which the device used to answer with
 479: #  fatal()+exit(1); #545 declines that read, so the control that "this launcher notices a dead child"
 480: #  now comes from the harness itself:
 481: #    H1  an unknown -E name: machine.c prints `Sorry, emulation "<name>" is unknown.` and main.c
 482: #        exits 1 BEFORE any prompt.  Rowed on NO prompt AND the diagnostic naming THIS input AND
 483: #        WIFEXITED && == 1 -- five different startup refusals all exit 1 with no prompt (measured,
 484: #        pass 1), so only the text clause discriminates.
 485: #    H2  the prologue stepped to its end, a FRESH `reg` with r3 bit 0 clear, r5 = 0x11223344,
 486: #        r6 = VENDOR and r7 = LOADBACK (it really ran), `quit`, then ONE MORE ordinary `reg`: it
 487: #        must fail with `dead` set BEFORE close(), and close() must reap exit 0.  A child dead
 488: #        BEFORE the prologue finished shows `next send fails` + `dead` IDENTICALLY (measured), so
 489: #        the fresh-reg values and exit == 0 are both load-bearing.
 490: H1_NAME = "no-such-machine-fbd5"
 491: 
 492: 
 493: def run_legs():
 494:     #  H1
 495:     s = Session(emul=H1_NAME)
 496:     prompt = s.wait(timeout=120)
 497:     end = s.close()
 498:     h1 = dict(prompt=prompt, noprompt=("GXemul>" not in s.buf),
 499:               named=('Sorry, emulation "%s" is unknown' % H1_NAME) in s.buf, **end)
 500:     #  H2
 501:     prog = MMU_OFF + prologue(*SENT_A) + [(0x48, NOP, NOPTXT, "landing pad")]
 502:     s = Session()
 503:     if not s.wait(timeout=120):
 504:         return dict(h1=h1, h2=dict(status="NO-PROMPT", **s.close()))
 505:     plant(s, prog)
 506:     bad, _, ex = step_and_check(s, prog, PROLOGUE_N)
 507:     r = s.regs((3, 5, 6, 7))
 508:     prologue_ok = (not bad and len(ex) == PROLOGUE_N and 3 in r and (r[3] & 1) == 0
 509:                    and r.get(5) == 0x11223344 and r.get(6) == VENDOR and r.get(7) == LOADBACK)
 510:     quit_rc = s.send("quit", timeout=10)
 511:     next_rc = s.send("reg", timeout=10)          # observed BEFORE close(); never short-circuited
 512:     dead_before_close = s.dead
 513:     end = s.close()
 514:     h2 = dict(status="SURVIVED" if prologue_ok else "PROLOGUE-FAILED", prologue_ok=prologue_ok,
 515:               quit_rc=quit_rc, next_rc=next_rc, dead_before_close=dead_before_close, **end)
 516:     return dict(h1=h1, h2=h2)
 517: 
 518: 
 519: def run_encodings():
 520:     """Arm E: sentinel 0 makes the poll compare EQUAL, so `step 1` walks BOTH loop
 521:     bodies and every planted word is checked as it EXECUTES."""
 522:     prog = MMU_OFF + prologue(*SENT_E) + LOOP + [LANDING]
 523:     s = Session()
 524:     if not s.wait(timeout=120):
 525:         return dict(status="NO-PROMPT", regs={}, badis=[], covered=set(), want=set(),
 526:                     nsteps=0, executed=[], **s.close())
 527:     plant(s, prog)
 528:     bad, covered, ex = step_and_check(s, prog, PROLOGUE_N + 2 * len(LOOP))
 529:     r = s.regs()
 530:     want = {CODE + off for off, *_ in prog if off != LANDING[0]}
 531:     status = "HOST-DIED" if s.dead else "SURVIVED"
 532:     end = s.close()
 533:     return dict(status=status, regs=r, badis=bad, covered=covered, want=want,
 534:                 nsteps=len(ex), executed=ex, **end)
 535: 
 536: 
 537: def main():
 538:     t_probe = time.time()
 539:     print("=" * 78)
 540:     print("fbpending witness -- %s" % BIN)
 633:     rA, rE, rT = A["regs"], E["regs"], T["regs"]
 634:     ranA = survived(A) and len(S) == SLICES
 635:     ranE = survived(E)
 636:     ranT = survived(T) and len(T["samples"]) == 1
 637: 
 638:     row("R0 every arm produced data", ranA and ranE and ranT and H2["status"] != "NO-PROMPT",
 639:         "; ".join("%s=%s exit=%s signal=%s" %
 640:                   (name, a["status"], a.get("exit"), a.get("signal"))
 641:                   for name, a in (("A", A), ("E", E), ("T", T), ("H2", H2)))
 642:         + "; A samples=%d/%d" % (len(S), SLICES))
 643:     row("C1 liveness: RAM through the same decode",
 644:         rA.get(5) == 0x11223344 and rE.get(5) == 0x11223344,
 645:         "A r5=0x%x  E r5=0x%x" % (rA.get(5, -1), rE.get(5, -1)))
 646:     row("C2 the MMU is OFF in the device arms",
 647:         (rA.get(3, 1) & 1) == 0 and (rE.get(3, 1) & 1) == 0,
 648:         "CP15 c1: A=0x%x E=0x%x (bit 0 must be clear)"
 649:         % (rA.get(3, -1), rE.get(3, -1)))
 650:     row("A1 device signature VENDOR_ID",
 651:         rA.get(6) == VENDOR and rE.get(6) == VENDOR,
 652:         "A r6=0x%x E r6=0x%x (expect 0x%x; RAM and absent both give 0)"
 653:         % (rA.get(6, -1), rE.get(6, -1), VENDOR))
 654:     row("A2 device signature: the handler's own 24-bit mask",
 655:         rA.get(7) == LOADBACK and rE.get(7) == LOADBACK,
 656:         "wrote 0x12345678, read A=0x%08x E=0x%08x (RAM would return 0x12345678)"
 657:         % (rA.get(7, -1), rE.get(7, -1)))
 658:     row("E  encodings: every planted word, as EXECUTED",
 659:         ranE and not E["badis"] and not (E["want"] - E["covered"]),
 660:         "%d mismatches, %d/%d offsets stepped"
 661:         % (len(E["badis"]), len(E["covered"]), len(E["want"])))
 662:     row("H1 startup leg: unknown -E named, no prompt, exit 1",
 663:         H1["prompt"] is False and H1.get("noprompt") is True and H1["named"]
 664:         and H1.get("exit") == 1 and H1.get("signal") is None,
 665:         "prompt=%s named=%s exit=%s signal=%s" %
 666:         (H1["prompt"], H1["named"], H1.get("exit"), H1.get("signal")))
 667:     row("H2 quit leg: verified prologue, next send dead before close, exit 0",
 668:         H2.get("prologue_ok") is True and H2.get("next_rc") is False
 669:         and H2.get("dead_before_close") is True and H2.get("exit") == 0 and H2.get("signal") is None
 670:         and survived(A),
 671:         "prologue=%s next_rc=%s dead=%s exit=%s signal=%s, A=%s" %
 672:         (H2.get("prologue_ok"), H2.get("next_rc"), H2.get("dead_before_close"),
 673:          H2.get("exit"), H2.get("signal"), A["status"]))
 674:     row("T  the MMU-ON control cannot see the device",
 675:         #  F2 (#539): the keys must be PRESENT -- None != VENDOR is True, so a register-parse miss
 676:         #  greened this row once survived() forbade the halt outcome.
 677:         ranT and 6 in rT and 7 in rT and rT[6] != VENDOR and rT[7] != LOADBACK,
 678:         "T r6=0x%x r7=0x%x" % (rT.get(6, -1), rT.get(7, -1)))
 679:     ratio = (S[0]["iters"] / S[0]["clob"]) if (S and S[0]["clob"]) else 0
 680:     row("D1 the ticks come from the DEVICE, not an absent read",
 681:         bool(S) and S[0]["clob"] > 0 and ratio >= 100,
 201:             emit(o, NOP, "arm T: MMU LEFT ON -- the negative control")
 202:     else:
 203:         p.append((0x00, 0xEE110F10, "mrc 15,0,r0,cr1,cr0,0", "read CP15 c1"))
 204:         p.append((0x04, 0xE3C00001, "bic r0,r0,#1", "clear MMU enable"))
 205:         p.append((0x08, 0xEE010F10, "mcr 15,0,r0,cr1,cr0,0", "MMU OFF -> VA == PA"))
 206:     p.append((0x0c, 0xEE113F10, "mrc 15,0,r3,cr1,cr0,0", "C2: CP15 c1 read BACK"))
 207:     emit(0x10, mov("r0", FB), "footbridge base")
 208:     emit(0x14, mov("r4", DATA), "scratch RAM")
 209:     emit(0x18, ldr("r5", "r4", K_LIVE), "C1 liveness, expect 0x11223344")
 210:     emit(0x1c, ldr("r6", "r0", 0), "A1 VENDOR_ID, expect 0x1011")
 211:     emit(0x20, ldr("r2", "r4", K_SRC), "0x12345678 out of RAM")
 212:     emit(0x24, st("r2", "r0", T_LOAD), "TIMER_x_LOAD <- 0x12345678")
 213:     emit(0x28, ldr("r7", "r0", T_LOAD), "A2 expect 0x00345678 (the handler's mask)")
 214:     emit(0x2c, ldr("r2", "r4", K_CON), "CTRL = TIMER_ENABLE|TIMER_FCLK_256")
 215:     emit(0x30, st("r2", "r0", T_CTRL), "TIMER_1_CONTROL <- 0x88")
 216:     emit(0x34, ldr("r2", "r4", K_L), "load 256")
 217:     emit(0x38, st("r2", "r0", T_LOAD), "TIMER_1_LOAD <- 256 => 762.94 Hz")
 218:     emit(0x3c, ldr("r8", "r4", K_SEN), "sentinel 0x00FFFFFF")
 219:     emit(0x40, ldr("ip", "r4", K_SPIN), "spin budget")
 220:     emit(0x44, mov("sl", 0), "drained count")
 221:     emit(0x48, mov("lr", 0), "accrual iterations")
 222:     emit(0x4c, mov("r1", 0), "")
 223:     emit(0x50, st("r1", "r4", O_PHASE), "PHASE = 0")
 224:     if disable_during_accrual:
 225:         emit(0x54, ldr("r2", "r4", K_COFF), "arm X: CTRL = 0")
 226:         emit(0x58, st("r2", "r0", T_CTRL),
 227:              "arm X: DISABLE.  Zeroes the counter ONCE and leaves the core timer running.")
 228:     else:
 229:         emit(0x54, NOP, "")
 230:         emit(0x58, NOP, "")
 231:     #  accrual loop -- publishes liveness and a fresh VENDOR_ID every iteration
 232:     emit(0x5c, add1("lr"), "ACCRUE: iterations++")
 233:     emit(0x60, st("lr", "r4", O_ITER), "publish iterations")
 234:     emit(0x64, ldr("r1", "r0", 0), "fresh VENDOR_ID read, every iteration")
 235:     emit(0x68, st("r1", "r4", O_VEND), "publish it")
 236:     p.append((0x6c, br(0xE, CODE + 0x5c, CODE + 0x6c)[0], None, "b ACCRUE"))
 237:     #  ---- DRAIN entry.  The harness sets pc here after the accrual free-run. ----
 238:     if disable_during_accrual:
 239:         emit(0x70, ldr("r2", "r4", K_CON), "arm X: re-enable")
 240:         emit(0x74, st("r2", "r0", T_CTRL),
 241:              "arm X: CONTROL <- 0x88.  reload_timer_value() does NOT clear the counter.")
 242:     else:
 243:         emit(0x70, NOP, "")
 244:         emit(0x74, NOP, "")
 245:     emit(0x78, ldr("r2", "r4", K_FRZ), "0x00FFFFFF")
 246:     emit(0x7c, st("r2", "r0", T_LOAD), "FREEZE: LOAD <- 0x00FFFFFF => 0.0116 Hz")
 247:     emit(0x80, mov("r1", 1), "")
 248:     emit(0x84, st("r1", "r4", O_PHASE), "PHASE = 1")
 249:     emit(0x88, ldr("sp", "r4", K_LIM), "drain limit")
 250:     emit(0x8c, st("r8", "r0", T_VALUE), "DLOOP: TIMER_1_VALUE <- sentinel")
 251:     emit(0x90, movr("fp", "ip"), "reset spin budget")
 252:     emit(0x94, ldr("r9", "r0", T_VALUE), "SPIN: read TIMER_1_VALUE")
 253:     emit(0x98, cmpr("r9", "r8"), "")
 254:     p.append((0x9c, br(0x1, CODE + 0xac, CODE + 0x9c)[0], None, "bne TICKSEEN"))
 255:     emit(0xa0, subs1("fp"), "")
 256:     p.append((0xa4, br(0x1, CODE + 0x94, CODE + 0xa4)[0], None, "bne SPIN"))
 257:     p.append((0xa8, br(0xE, CODE + 0xc8, CODE + 0xa8)[0], None,
 258:               "budget expired, sentinel intact -> backlog is 0"))
 259:     emit(0xac, st("r9", "r4", O_LASTV), "TICKSEEN: publish the value the tick wrote")
 260:     emit(0xb0, st("r1", "r0", T_CLEAR), "TIMER_1_CLEAR -- one decrement")
 261:     emit(0xb4, add1("sl"), "")
 262:     emit(0xb8, st("sl", "r4", O_DRAIN), "publish the drained count")
 263:     emit(0xbc, cmpr("sl", "sp"), "")
 264:     p.append((0xc0, br(0xA, CODE + 0xdc, CODE + 0xc0)[0], None, "bge OVER"))
 265:     p.append((0xc4, br(0xE, CODE + 0x8c, CODE + 0xc4)[0], None, "b DLOOP"))
 266:     emit(0xc8, mov("r1", 3), "DONE:")
 267:     emit(0xcc, st("r1", "r4", O_PHASE), "PHASE = 3")
 268:     emit(0xd0, ldr("r1", "r0", 0), "final VENDOR_ID")
 269:     emit(0xd4, st("r1", "r4", O_VEND), "")
 270:     p.append((0xd8, br(0xE, CODE + 0xd8, CODE + 0xd8)[0], None, "HALT"))
 271:     emit(0xdc, mov("r1", 4), "OVER:")
 272:     emit(0xe0, st("r1", "r4", O_PHASE), "PHASE = 4 -- drain limit hit")
 273:     p.append((0xe4, br(0xE, CODE + 0xd8, CODE + 0xe4)[0], None, "b HALT"))
 274:     return p
 275: 
 276: 
 277: DRAIN_ENTRY = CODE + 0x70
 278: PROLOGUE_N = 21          # 0x00 .. 0x50 inclusive
 279: 
 280: 
 281: # ------------------------------------------------------------------ session
 282: class Session:
 599: #  #545: THE HARNESS LEGS, replacing arm K.  Arm K read +0x18c, which the device used to answer with
 600: #  fatal()+exit(1); #545 declines that read, so the control that "this launcher notices a dead child"
 601: #  now comes from the harness itself:
 602: #    H1  an unknown -E name: machine.c prints `Sorry, emulation "<name>" is unknown.` and main.c
 603: #        exits 1 BEFORE any prompt.  Rowed on NO prompt AND the diagnostic naming THIS input AND
 604: #        WIFEXITED && == 1 -- five different startup refusals all exit 1 with no prompt (measured,
 605: #        pass 1), so only the text clause discriminates.
 606: #    H2  the prologue stepped to its end, a FRESH `reg` with r3 bit 0 clear, r5 = 0x11223344,
 607: #        r6 = VENDOR and r7 = LOADBACK (it really ran), `quit`, then ONE MORE ordinary `reg`: it
 608: #        must fail with `dead` set BEFORE close(), and close() must reap exit 0.  A child dead
 609: #        BEFORE the prologue finished shows `next send fails` + `dead` IDENTICALLY (measured), so
 610: #        the fresh-reg values and exit == 0 are both load-bearing.
 611: H1_NAME = "no-such-machine-fbd5"
 612: 
 613: 
 614: def run_legs():
 615:     #  H1
 616:     s = Session(emul=H1_NAME)
 617:     prompt = s.wait(timeout=120)
 618:     end = s.close()
 619:     h1 = dict(prompt=prompt, noprompt=("GXemul>" not in s.buf),
 620:               named=('Sorry, emulation "%s" is unknown' % H1_NAME) in s.buf, **end)
 621:     #  H2
 622:     prog = build()
 623:     s = Session()
 624:     if not s.wait(timeout=120):
 625:         return dict(h1=h1, h2=dict(status="NO-PROMPT", **s.close()))
 626:     plant(s, prog)
 627:     bad, cov, ex = step_and_check(s, prog, PROLOGUE_N)
 628:     r = s.regs((3, 5, 6, 7))
 629:     prologue_ok = (not bad and len(ex) == PROLOGUE_N and 3 in r and (r[3] & 1) == 0
 630:                    and r.get(5) == 0x11223344 and r.get(6) == VENDOR and r.get(7) == LOADBACK)
 631:     quit_rc = s.send("quit", timeout=10)
 632:     next_rc = s.send("reg", timeout=10)          # observed BEFORE close(); never short-circuited
 633:     dead_before_close = s.dead
 634:     end = s.close()
 635:     h2 = dict(status="SURVIVED" if prologue_ok else "PROLOGUE-FAILED", prologue_ok=prologue_ok,
 636:               quit_rc=quit_rc, next_rc=next_rc, dead_before_close=dead_before_close, **end)
 637:     return dict(h1=h1, h2=h2)
 638: 
 639: 
 640: def main():
 641:     t0 = time.time()
 642:     print("=" * 78)
 643:     print("fbpending DRAIN PROBE -- %s" % BIN)
 644:     print("  TIMER %d (index %d, base 0x%x): FCLK_256, load %d -> %.2f Hz.  accrual %.1f s"
 645:           % (TIMER_NR + 1, TIMER_NR, TBASE, LOAD_RUN, RATE, ACCRUE_S))
 646:     print("  UNCAPPED prediction: backlog = %.0f" % (RATE * ACCRUE_S))
 647:     print("=" * 78)
 648: 
 649:     skip = dict(label="(not run)", status="SKIPPED", regs={}, badis=[],
 650:                 covered=set(), executed=[], out=None, mid=None, phase=None)
 651:     LEGS = (run_legs() if not ONLY else
 714: 
 715:     rD, rX, rT = D.get("regs", {}), X.get("regs", {}), T.get("regs", {})
 716:     ranD = survived(D) and D.get("out") is not None
 717:     ranX = survived(X) and X.get("out") is not None
 718:     row("R0 every arm produced data",
 719:         ranD and ranX and survived(T) and survived(E) and H2["status"] != "NO-PROMPT",
 720:         "; ".join("%s=%s exit=%s signal=%s" %
 721:                   (name, a["status"], a.get("exit"), a.get("signal"))
 722:                   for name, a in (("D", D), ("X", X), ("T", T), ("E", E), ("H2", H2))))
 723:     row("C1 liveness: RAM through the same decode",
 724:         rD.get(5) == 0x11223344 and rX.get(5) == 0x11223344,
 725:         "D r5=0x%x  X r5=0x%x" % (rD.get(5, -1), rX.get(5, -1)))
 726:     row("C2 the MMU is OFF in the device arms",
 727:         (rD.get(3, 1) & 1) == 0 and (rX.get(3, 1) & 1) == 0,
 728:         "CP15 c1: D=0x%x X=0x%x" % (rD.get(3, -1), rX.get(3, -1)))

# #545 pass-2 repair -- the mutants the panel named after the X1-X3 repair, each ONE edit on the FIXED dev_footbridge.c.
#   python mkmut_p2b.py <tree-root> <mutant>          every find must match EXACTLY ONCE
import io, sys, os
root, mut = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
p = os.path.join(root, 'src', 'devices', 'dev_footbridge.c')
s = io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', newline='').read()
def rep(old, new):
    global s
    assert s.count(old) == 1, (mut, old[:70], s.count(old))
    s = s.replace(old, new)

EXIT = '\t\t\texit(1);\n'
if mut == 'Z1':      # codex R1: the IRQ_STATUS latch guard conditioned on the data
    rep('else if (!d->warned_irq_status_w) {', 'else if (!d->warned_irq_status_w && idata) {')
elif mut == 'Z1b':   # codex R1 at FIQ_RAW_STATUS
    rep('else if (!d->warned_fiq_raw_w) {', 'else if (!d->warned_fiq_raw_w && idata) {')
elif mut == 'Z2':    # codex R1: the 18c read latch guard conditioned on irq_enable
    rep('} else if (!d->warned_irq_clear_r) {', '} else if (!d->warned_irq_clear_r && d->irq_enable) {')
elif mut == 'Z3':    # codex R2: the IRQ_STATUS direction test rewritten on the data
    rep('\tcase IRQ_STATUS:\n\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_READ)', '\tcase IRQ_STATUS:\n\t\tif (!idata)')
elif mut == 'Z4':    # codex R2: the EnableClear direction test rewritten on the data
    rep('\tcase IRQ_ENABLE_CLEAR:\n\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {', '\tcase IRQ_ENABLE_CLEAR:\n\t\tif (idata) {')
elif mut == 'Z5':    # codex R3: the IRQ_STATUS read's status field swapped for the FIQ one
    rep('odata = d->irq_status & d->irq_enable;', 'odata = d->fiq_status & d->irq_enable;')
elif mut == 'Z6':    # codex R3: the IRQ_STATUS read's enable field swapped for fiq_status
    rep('odata = d->irq_status & d->irq_enable;', 'odata = d->irq_status & d->fiq_status;')
elif mut == 'Z7':    # consolidation F4: a legal VENDOR_ID read clears the IRQ_STATUS latch
    rep('\tcase VENDOR_ID:\n', '\tcase VENDOR_ID:\n\t\td->warned_irq_status_w = 0;\n')
elif mut == 'Z7b':   # consolidation F4: a legal IRQEnableSet WRITE clears the FIQ_RAW_STATUS latch
    rep('\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {\n\t\t\td->irq_enable |= idata;\n',
        '\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {\n\t\t\td->warned_fiq_raw_w = 0;\n\t\t\td->irq_enable |= idata;\n')
elif mut == 'Z8':    # consolidation F6: a NARROW declined 18c read leaks irq_enable
    rep('\t\t} else if (!d->warned_irq_clear_r) {', '\t\t} else if ((len < 4 ? (odata = d->irq_enable) : 0), !d->warned_irq_clear_r) {')
elif mut == 'Z9':    # codex R4 (GUEST-only): IRQ_STATUS and IRQ_RAW_STATUS exchange their announce texts
    rep('"[ WARNING: footbridge write to irq status? "\n\t\t\t    "(once) ]\\n"', '"[ footbridge write to irq_raw_status "\n\t\t\t    "(once) ]\\n"')
    rep('fatal("[ footbridge write to irq_raw_status (once) ]\\n");', 'fatal("[ WARNING: footbridge write to irq status? (once) ]\\n");')
elif mut in ('M1b', 'M1c', 'M1d', 'M1e'):   # floor (b) at the other four sites: verbatim reinstatement of exit(1)
    site = {'M1b': 'warned_irq_status_w', 'M1c': 'warned_irq_raw_w', 'M1d': 'warned_irq_clear_r', 'M1e': 'warned_fiq_status_w'}[mut]
    i = s.index('d->%s = 1;\n' % site)
    j = s.index(');\n', s.index('fatal(', i)) + 3
    assert s.count('d->%s = 1;\n' % site) == 1
    s = s[:j] + EXIT + s[j:]
else:
    raise SystemExit('unknown mutant ' + mut)

  38: # `#539` (n6, round 1 of the footbridge campaign): a documented read-only register was answered correctly and then the process was ended -- the exit is gone, the value stays, and the probe that used that exit as its own failability control moved one register over
  39: 
  40: **The defect.**  `dev_footbridge.c`, `DEVICE_ACCESS(footbridge)`, `case IRQ_ENABLE_SET` (offset `0x188` on the
  41: `-E cats` DC21285): a guest READ computed `odata = d->irq_enable` -- the right answer -- and then called `fatal("...
  42: ENABLE SET? ...")` and `exit(1)`.  The DC21285 datasheet (Intel 278115-001, `_scratchpad/dc21285.txt`) says the
  43: register at 188h is IRQEnable, "read-only ... can only be changed by writing to the EnableSet and EnableClear
  44: registers", reset value 0 (Table 7-10 at `:6826`, 7.3.31 at `:6913-6915`, `:6924`).  So the model already held the
  45: right value and a legal guest load ended the emulator.  Option-set member 4, ACCEPT: the `fatal()` and the `exit(1)`
  46: are deleted, `odata = d->irq_enable;` is kept, and a `/* #539 */` comment quotes the datasheet.  No latch and no
  47: message, because there is no event to announce; a bounded announce would have printed "unexpected" about a read
  48: that is expected.  `est/` carries the byte-identical twin.

  50: **Witness (rung 3, on the committed tree).**  The new arm E of `regress/footbridge_sites_probe.py` -- real guest
  51: `ldr`/`str`/`ldrb`/`ldrh` through the real decode on an unmodified `cats` after the MMU-off prologue -- HOST-DIES at
  52: its first word on HEAD a11eaac (exit 1, `ENABLE SET` diagnostic; `regress/artifacts/n6/killtable_20260919/

  54: 19/19).  Pre-fix, the death diagnostic is the device signature and the step echo is liveness; post-fix the arm
  55: carries its own C1 (RAM) and A1 (VENDOR_ID 0x1011) because E0 = 0 is also what an ABSENT device returns.  The Opus 5
  56: seat had measured the same red/green in its own clone on pass 1 (`regress/artifacts/n6/opus_p1_20260919/
  57: e_prefix.raw`, `e_postfix.raw`), before the round's own probe existed.
  58: 
  59: **The detector -- seven ACCEPT rows in arm E, execution order E0,E1,E2,E3,E6,E5, plus E4.**  E0 reads IRQEnable
  60: before any write (0, the reset value); E1 writes P = 0x00002002 to Set and reads P back; E2 writes Q = 0x60000000 and
  61: reads P|Q (pins `|=`; bit 30 is set so a diagnostic kept for the top bits would fire here); E3 `ldrb` returns the low
  62: byte 0x02 and E6 `ldrh` the low half 0x2002 (the CSR drives D[31:0] and the SA-110 takes the lane, `dc21285.txt:
  63: 1651-1653` -- architecture, not policy); E5 writes P to Clear and reads Q back; E4 requires no footbridge diagnostic
  64: in the arm and exit 0.  E6 runs before E5 because its value is true only while P is set -- the pass-1 panel caught
  65: the first draft's order, which would have reddened a correct build.  P and Q are loaded from RAM (P is not a
  66: rotated ARM immediate; Q is, and is loaded the same way for uniformity); `ldrh` immediates cap at 255, so the halfword read is `add sl,r0,#0x180` + `ldrh fp,[sl,#8]`;
  67: the probe's register parser now reads r0-r9, sl, fp, ip (the emulator's own spellings, `cpu_arm.h` ARM_REG_NAMES).
  68: Every planted word is checked against the disassembly the emulator printed as it stepped it (row D1).
  69: `gate_arm.sh` pins the E rows three ways: count 7, E0 `got=0x00000000`, E2 `got=0x60002002`.
  70:

A one-token change to a newly added latch assignment escapes the reviewed composite by inspection. The repair checks first accesses without establishing that those accesses actually latch. I found no defect in the unchanged product behavior; the blocker is the claimed detector coverage.

For Q1, I would change these dispositions:

- **F4 — OVERTURN “repaired” as a complete disposition.** X3’s added enable writes carry zero. A latch cleared only by a nonzero legal write still escapes.
- **F6 — REFINE.** X1 repairs the first narrow declined read’s value. Repeated narrow read values and narrow handler return codes remain unchecked.
- **F7 — REFINE.** The named guard mutations are covered, but write-side enable dependencies and data-dependent latch assignments remain open.
- **R4 — REFINE.** NO catches the measured text exchange. Ordering diagnostics does not establish which access emitted each diagnostic.
- **F34 — REFINE.** The working gate contains the correction, but [CHANGELOG.md:227](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/CHANGELOG.md:227) still claims the distinct-green-ID check shipped with #539. The promised record correction is absent from this tree.
- **F40 — OVERTURN the appended correction’s remaining claim.** [PRETABLE.out:119](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/artifacts/fbd5/killtable_20260919/PRETABLE.out:119) still says its mtime proves ordering for text preceding a heading. A modified file’s mtime cannot establish when any particular portion was written.

For Q2, the smallest escape I found is **one token** at [the FIQ_RAW_STATUS latch assignment](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_footbridge.c:644): replace `1` with `idata`.

```c
d->warned_fiq_raw_w = idata;
```

The first zero write announces but leaves the latch zero. A second zero write announces again.

Every relevant current test misses this:

- P5’s first and repeated writes use `0x70`, which sets a nonzero latch.
- Guest N5 uses `0x40`, also nonzero.
- X1 and X3 use `0x70`.
- X4 supplies zero, checks one announcement and four register fields, then frees the instance. It never repeats the zero write.

The expected detectors are **P5 “#2 announced nothing more (latched)”** and **X4’s zero-data case**, but their distinguishing conditions never occur together. This directly alters an assignment introduced by the fix. It is a source-traced escape, not a compiled measurement.

Your three proposed directions also remain open by inspection:

- **Enable-dependent announcement:** add `&& d->irq_enable` to the IRQ_STATUS write latch guard. P9, X1, X3, X4’s write case, and guest N1 all have IRQEnable `0x30`. X4 zeros IRQEnable only for the Clear **read**. A first declined write with IRQEnable zero remains untested.
- **Nonzero legal write re-arms:** insert `if (idata) d->warned_irq_status_w = 0;` in the legal IRQ_ENABLE_CLEAR write branch. X3 writes zero. P8 writes nonzero after its last IRQ_STATUS decline. Guest arm E has no declines, and arm S has no intervening Clear write.
- **Narrow decline changes interrupt output:** an assertion conditional on `len < 4`, inside FIQ_RAW_STATUS’s first-decline body, is invisible. X1 reaches it but never inspects callback counts. P5 measures callbacks only for its first word access; its later narrow accesses are already latched. Guest N5 uses words.

For Q3, the new comparisons generally establish their specific sampled observations. Several broader claims still exceed those observations:

1. **X1 never checks that a narrow first access remains latched.** Replacing the same latch assignment’s RHS with `len == 4` also escapes: X1 announces once and frees each instance; P5 establishes the latch with a word first. Repeat each fresh narrow access before freeing it. The gap is visible in [X1’s lifecycle](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/diff_footbridge.c:740).

2. **X2 cannot distinguish halfword status data from byte-truncated data.** All four status/enable seeds fit in one byte. Returning `d->irq_status & 0xff` from IRQ_RAW_STATUS preserves every current expected result, including the guest’s zero. Use additional seeds with nonzero upper bytes. The reseeding successfully separates field substitutions; it does not establish width preservation.

3. **The narrow-read repair covers the unlatched path only.** A Clear read that leaks IRQEnable only when already latched and `len < 4` escapes: P3 performs those accesses without checking their returned buffers; X1 checks buffers only on fresh instances. Capture the return code and read value after every relevant access.

4. **NO proves text order, not access attribution.** [The probe](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/footbridge_sites_probe.py:599) orders diagnostics collected from the entire session, including teardown. Moving a diagnostic to another execution point can preserve that order. The existing per-step `seg` provides a straightforward place to associate diagnostics with instructions. The measured Z9 result stands; the stronger attribution claim does not.

5. **The new offline pins repeat the duplicate-line weakness.** [gate_offline.sh:595](C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/gate_offline.sh:595) counts 78 P lines and 38 X lines without checking distinct subrows.

   I evaluated those predicates against an in-memory copy of `diff_FIX.log`, replacing the X5 state-preservation line with a duplicate X5 announcement line. P remained 78, X remained 38, and the identity, zero-failure summary, and PASS predicates remained satisfied; distinct X lines fell to 37. Conversely, replacing guest S2 with duplicate S1 made the repaired ARM distinct-ID predicate fail as intended. Pin the expected set of individual P/X row IDs.

For Q4, two further record defects survived the adjudication:

- Table 3 contains **35 distinct mutants**, plus FIX—not 34.
- The sites probe still says widths are covered by “P-rows” at line 106 and still calls the N counts “ACCESS-LOCAL” at lines 259–260. Those comments preserve claims the repair otherwise corrected.

Before sign-off, the bounded measurements I want are the `1 → idata` and `1 → len == 4` assignment mutations, the three candidates above, and a latched narrow Clear-read value mutation. Extend the rows to inspect repeated fresh zero/narrow accesses, nonzero intervening enable writes, returned values/codes, and interrupt effects. Then verify the distinct-subrow pins and complete the already-planned operator ARM gate run. That final gate result and the eventual staged commit remain unsettled.
